# rc1 Windows exact-package verification — 2026-10-29

**PASS within the selected Windows/provider scope: 1,448/1,448 required case checks across seven stages.** All stages finalized successfully with stable inputs; every complete case/retained-file inventory was independently verified. The selected archives and extracted member bytes remained unchanged. No code or test correction and no retry was needed.

This is a standalone report only, as requested. Existing documentation, roadmap, release checklist, candidate manifest and top-level README were not edited. Raw runs and local orchestration remain under ignored `target/`; the machine-readable appendix makes the result understandable without those local files or Git history. This report does not authorize publication.

## Exact inputs

| Archive | Bytes | SHA-256 |
|---|---:|---|
| `fsr-sdk-0.3.0.crate` | 318967 | `e198d6f87938bfe3f321fdd61355ba0ec8cb4b133633f72ad83e3713fbfed766` |
| `fsr-sdk-sys-0.1.2.crate` | 43576 | `2137546b831d71b1b5e779273f8389ad51bcb28f16a92141746db7c5e57ce042` |

Both archives come from the checked-in `release-candidates/0.3.0/rc1/` pair. The frozen manifest records clean source revision `6e1c93ed2ba85561faafa7f18f5d175d2caf0ffa`, SDK v2.3.0 and MSRV 1.88.0. Every stage selected both paths explicitly, used a checksum-verified isolated local registry and checked ordinary archived members against the checkout. No package was regenerated or substituted. The common source/SDK/archive content identity is `e2e0447ffde0ca00b6152a2923d8636b10e75a98428334d4742afd4a91f18a41`; per-stage configuration identities remain separate. Cross-host comparisons should match the two archive hashes, not executable hashes or whole host-input digests.

## Results

| Stage | Required checks | Scope |
|---|---:|---|
| `rc1-package-core` | 630/630 | Stable/MSRV seven-feature extracted-package matrix; Clippy/Rustdoc, registry-only consumers and complete workflows; independent ABI, core GPU/Configure/sharpening, callbacks and production CB exchange |
| `native-motion-scale` | 106/106 | Both owners/providers; render/display motion, signed/asymmetric scaling, jitter, active output and UAV inputs |
| `native-formats` | 170/170 | Typed colour/output, R16F depth and supported generator inputs; independent C++ comparisons |
| `native-depth-formats` | 86/86 | Typed/typeless D32/D32S8, reversed depth, dynamic extents, depth/stencil/shader readbacks and C++ comparisons |
| `native-bgra-srgb` | 106/106 | BGRA/sRGB main and generator views, UAV inputs, fenced native calls and resource integrity |
| `native-resource-formats` | 284/284 | Remaining colour/depth/motion/exposure/mask/output roles and supported provider-conditioned generation |
| `native-variable-output` | 66/66 | Active output within fixed maxima, motion encodings, sharpening, both owners/providers and independent input/output audits |
| **Total** | **1,448/1,448** | Selected rc1 Windows coverage |

The first stage combines the existing `release-artifacts` inventory (594 cases) and `release-coverage` inventory (70 cases). Their 34 shared case IDs execute once; one final integrity case depends on every unique case, yielding 630 checks. Existing case actions/oracles are unchanged. The remaining six stages use the ordinary named profiles with both archive arguments, which imply artifact mode. Standalone source CI/MSRV and the earlier source-native qualification were not repeated or counted here. Nested Cargo tests and intentional ignored tests are not added to the required-case total. Expected compile failures remain successful controls only when the required diagnostic/exit contract is met.

## Execution and equipment

The batch ran from `2026-10-29T16:47:58.975782+00:00` to `2026-10-29T17:30:18.815060+00:00` (UTC). Stable is Rust/Cargo 1.99.0 on Windows 11 x64/MSVC; MSRV cases explicitly use Rust 1.88.0. Builds use dev/test debug `0`, `CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0` and warning-denied Rustdoc. Cargo stays locked/offline. The local SDK root is `target/sdk-windows-verification/v2.3.0/Kits/FidelityFX`, with adapter index 0. Exact tools, command environments, source/external file hashes, archive member inventories and native trust observations are retained below.

Observed native identity lines (provider IDs are metadata for the queried runtime/device, not universal capabilities):


The SDK headers remain pinned to v2.3.0 commit `60f4ea81909200d8542eca14dccb2628b763a9a3`. Native setup checks the pinned loader/upscaler DLL hashes and reports Windows Authenticode status `Valid`; detailed signatures are in the appendix. This is not a general provider/threading safety certificate or a claim about Proton trust policy. The callback cases check production C/Rust signatures and by-value CB exchange on Stable/MSRV, plus the selected actual-provider callback/dispatch sequence.

## Integrity and reproduction

The rc1 archive hashes were checked against the manifest before and after every stage. Registry setup rejects unsafe/duplicate/nonportable members and unwanted runtime/build material, verifies licences and package contracts, and extracts selected bytes without patching them. The final integrity cases reject changed archives, changed/extra extracted files and symlinks. Each finalized attempt was independently checked with `verify_run`; original run/inventory digests are retained below. All pre-existing tracked file hashes still match the pre-run snapshot, including top-level README SHA-256 `8440ecc1843cba91fbd153a31b7394fa949d2edbd4758c79f055711588988208`.

To reproduce a named feature stage from the repository root, use the same pair and a fresh output directory, for example:

```sh
python tools/verification/run.py run native-variable-output --require-passed --native --sdk-root target/sdk-windows-verification/v2.3.0/Kits/FidelityFX --adapter-index 0 --wrapper-archive release-candidates/0.3.0/rc1/fsr-sdk-0.3.0.crate --sys-archive release-candidates/0.3.0/rc1/fsr-sdk-sys-0.1.2.crate --output-root target/rc1-windows-new
```

The exact de-duplicating orchestration script is embedded in the appendix. Save it under a new empty ignored output directory and invoke it from the repository root with the recorded prerequisites/environment; never reopen an existing attempt. Alternatively, the standard `release-artifacts` and `release-coverage` commands cover the same core union but repeat the 34 shared cases. The appendix retains all case IDs and all 1627 command records in lossless argument/environment tables, with a decoding rule. Paths are sanitized; outcomes, native codes, provider IDs, hashes and required diagnostics are preserved.

## Limits

- This is the selected Windows x64/MSVC + DX12 cell and recorded GPU/driver/provider combinations. It establishes no native Linux/Vulkan support, new Proton result or universal hardware/provider compatibility.
- Positive generator coverage follows the protocol-selected 3.1.5 routes. The historical acknowledgement-without-write outcomes on default `4.1.2 *`, 4.1.1 and 2.3.4 are not retested or promoted to passing generator support here.
- Heap ABI/controlled calls are distinct from SDK heap invocation. Callback quiescence, general native failure recovery, arbitrary-thread compatibility and pending-GPU lifetime guarantees remain their documented conditional/unproved contracts. Passing readbacks do not establish image quality or universal access/numerical safety.
- No GBV stall investigation, deferred temporal/image-quality experiment, timing benchmark, P08 reconciliation, publication simulation, commit, push or publication was performed. Those separate release gates are not closed by this Windows report.
- Original diagnostics follow the existing producer retention policies. Successful resource-format child payloads may be discarded after evaluation; hashes cannot reconstruct removed frames. No complete image corpus or full export is checked in.

command=['tools/verification/run.py','run',name,'--require-passed','--native','--sdk-root',str(sdk),'--adapter-index','0','--wrapper-archive',str(candidate/'fsr-sdk-0.3.0.crate'),'--sys-archive',str(candidate/'fsr-sdk-sys-0.1.2.crate'),'--output-root',str(root)]\n print('Starting '+name+' ('+str(len(cases))+' cases)',flush=True)\n if name=='rc1-package-core':\n  opts=parser().parse_args(['run','release-artifacts']+command[3:])\n  directory=fresh(root,name)\n  r=Runner(Path.cwd(),directory,opts,env=env)\n  r.env['CARGO_TARGET_DIR']=str(Path('target/verification-build/current').resolve())\n  headers=json.loads(Path('tools/native-headers.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['headers']\n  extra=[('sdk/'+item['path'],sdk/item['path']) for item in headers]\n  extra += [('sdk/signedbin/'+p.name,p) for p in sorted((sdk/'signedbin').glob('amd_fidelityfx_*.dll'))]\n  extra += [('wrapper_archive',candidate/'fsr-sdk-0.3.0.crate'),('sys_archive',candidate/'fsr-sdk-sys-0.1.2.crate')]\n  r.state['external_inputs']=extra\n  code=r.execute(cases,name,require_passed=True)\n  command=['python',str(Path(__file__).relative_to(Path.cwd()) if Path(__file__).is_absolute() else Path(__file__))]\n else:\n  with (root/(name+'.log')).open('x',encoding='utf-8',newline='\\n') as out:\n   child=subprocess.run([sys.executable]+command,env=env,stdout=out,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW)\n  code=child.returncode\n  attempts=list(root.glob('*-'+name+'-*/run.json'));assert len(attempts)==1\n  directory=attempts[0].parent\n summary=verify_run(directory)\n row={'profile':name,'command':command,'exit_code':code,'attempt':directory.relative_to(Path.cwd()).as_posix() if directory.is_absolute() else directory.as_posix(),'status':summary['status'],'completed':summary['completed'],'inputs_unchanged':summary['inputs_unchanged'],'inventory_verified':True,'run_sha256':hashlib.sha256((directory/'run.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'inventory_sha256':hashlib.sha256((directory/'artifacts.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest()}\n index['runs'].append(row);save();print(json.dumps(row),flush=True)\n if code or summary['status']!='passed':\n  index.update(status='failed',reason='Stage failed; later stages not started');save();break\nelse:index['status']='finished'\nverify_pair()\nindex['finished_utc']=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat();save()\nraise SystemExit(0 if index['status']=='finished' else
